Text: A sequence is a finite order of containers holding realized evaluator values. It is an ordinary value with identity; its current held values can change while it stays the same sequence. Excludes: Losing one held value on passage, returning a distinct sequence solely from passage through identity, or silently replacing a handle by its contents row. | EV-004 | both; B:16; R:58–61; D:12; P:45–47,51,65 | Return a case of a fixed two-case identity choice, preserve sequence identity across mutation and distinct fresh identities at equal contents, and add row/case/set canonical-equality cases. | A:211; `same` gives a case of `truth` (K-8); identity of sequences by K-1; both +0043 | none; OPEN builtin identity, payload and scoped-sequence witness | changed | R16 Standing: draft — PROPOSED K-8 truth carrier attached to the retained same-identity law, under supplied identity/interning premises. Text: PROPOSED `same` returns primitive-produced `Case(truth,1,Row())` exactly when its two operands are one node, and `Case(truth,0,Row())` otherwise. For sequence handles, the premise is equality of the supplied stable sequence identities, not equality of current contents. For immutable values, coherent equal-node interning is an explicit obligation. This result law selects no K-1 scope/hash/supply recipe and supplies no physical interning implementation. Case: PROPOSED Supply distinct sequence identities `alpha != beta` for admitted handles `s` and `t`, both holding the single value `0`. `P(x)=Row(same(s,s),same(s,t))` on input `0` successfully yields `Row(Case(truth,1,Row()),Case(truth,0,Row()))`. An alias of `s` replaces either occurrence of `s` without changing these results. A valid mutation of what `s` holds likewise leaves the identity observations unchanged. This fixture supplies its identities; it does not construct them. Excludes: PROPOSED A natural `1`/`0` carrier, truth cases with nonempty primitive-produced payloads, yes for distinct supplied `alpha` and `beta` merely because both contents are `0`, or no for two occurrences of `s` after its contents change. | EV-010 | this proposal; B:22; R:130–133; R2:904; P:51,56–63 | Change the three-tag result family to three cases of one fixed builtin choice, define the internal global order separately and settle whether public compare exposes it. | A:217; `compare` stays on naturals and gives a case of `order` (K-8); the global order is internal (K-3); G +0043 | none; OPEN public domain, builtin mapping, order and comparison bound | changed | R16 Standing: draft — PROPOSED K-8 natural-domain/result-carrier core with the natural equation supplied conditionally by EV-117; no public heterogeneous comparison or adoption. Text: PROPOSED Public `compare` has naturals as its valid ordinary operand domain and returns a case of the completely bound built-in `order/3`, with its primitive-result empty-row payload. The internal K-3 order does not thereby become public comparison for bytes, rows, cases, sets or sequence handles. This domain statement supplies no wrong-shaped-argument result or refusal policy. Conditional on EV-117, compare(x,y) returns exactly Case((order,3),0,Row()) for x<y, Case((order,3),1,Row()) for x=y, and Case((order,3),2,Row()) for x>y. Case: Conditional on EV-117's DRAFT equation, P(x)=compare(1,2) on input 0, if the full run completes, outputs exactly Case((order,3),0,Row()). With operands already obtained its proposed primitive A/R/T/W is 3/0/0/1, total 4: remaining operation fuel 4 completes, while 3 stops unchanged with no primitive result. No sufficient complete-program fuel is supplied. Excludes: PROPOSED An unwrapped natural `1`, a case of truth/2 or another choice, an ordinal outside `0..2`, or a nonempty payload as this valid-domain primitive result. This record draws no outcome conclusion for `compare(B[],B[])` or any other operand outside the stated public domain. Destination: EV-010. | R16 residuals: The natural equation and primitive invoice are now DRAFT in EV-117; wrong-kind values still use EV-084/092. | EV-011 | this proposal; B:23; R:142–145; P:51 | Change divmod's tagged pair to row(quotient,remainder), amend its excluded shape accordingly and retain E-18 arithmetic calibration. | A:218; `divmod` gives a row (K-8); G +0043 | C8 for a zero divisor; OPEN E-2 zero-divisor default and E-3 costs | changed | R16 Standing: draft — reviewed conditional divmod equation/invoice proposal; the inherited row carrier remains; no adoption of positive equation or tariff. Text: PROPOSED For admitted naturals x,d with d>0, divmod(x,d)=Row(q,r), the unique natural pair satisfying x=q*d+r and 0<=r<d. The immutable two-field row has quotient first and remainder second, not a case wrapper or mutable sequence. Let a=ell(x), b=ell(d), Rq=max(1,a-b+1), Rr=min(a,b). Under N15 use (A,R,T,W)=(a+b,Rq+Rr+3,0,Rq*b). Rq bounds the quotient, Rr the remainder and 3 the two-field row; W counts prospective quotient-position/divisor-position pairs, regardless of implementation speed. Pay all components before constructing the quotient, remainder or row; all are built or none. Wrong kind is classified first under Rule E; d=0 instead uses EV-071. This is the missing proposed universal equation, not something forced by the old (7,3) calibration. Case: PROPOSED Obtained-operand cases, with result; A/R/T/W; total: D01 divmod(7,3)=Row(2,1);5/7/0/4;16. D02 divmod(0,5)=Row(0,0);4/5/0/3;12. D03 divmod(5,7)=Row(0,5);6/7/0/3;16. D04 divmod(8,1)=Row(8,0);5/8/0/4;17. D05 divmod(7,7)=Row(1,0);6/7/0/3;16. D06 divmod(2^64,2^32)=Row(2^32,0);98/69/0/1089;1256. D07 divmod(2^128-1,2^64-1)=Row(2^64+1,0);192/132/0/4160;4484. D08 divmod(2^64-1,2^64)=Row(0,2^64-1);129/68/0/65;262. D11 divmod(Row(),3)=none;3/0/0/1;4 by actual-part Rule E. Every threshold has N15's one-less stop control. D09/D10 are in EV-071; these are operation-stage cases, not whole-program budgets. Excludes: PROPOSED Rounding the quotient other than down, a positive-divisor remainder outside 0<=r<d, swapped fields, a case/sequence carrier, a discount for zero/short/existing results, machine-word W, second payment for the same work, overflowed invoices or construction before full payment. This rule does not apply the positive remainder bound at d=0 or assign zero's tariff; EV-071 does that separately. Destination: EV-011. | R16 residuals: PROPOSED R15 supplies the positive-divisor equation/invoice and wrong-kind operation-stage calibration conditionally; EV-071 separately supplies the zero result and proposed zero tariff. E-2/E-18 are narrowed, not closed. Row admission/identity/bytes (E-4/E-14/E-16), complete integration and meter assurance (E-3/E-12/E-27), unit/work bounds, positive T and unavailable-bound witnesses remain open. Text: For completed calls eligible under the proposed conservative transitive state-free gate EV-047, memoization keys a completed unary call by its argument's evaluator identity. SOURCE — Computing a term once and reusing its result is permitted only when the term makes nothing new, has no effect, has no open choice, consumes nothing use-once, and has a result that may be copied. State-freedom alone is insufficient. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json; OQ1 A with Arch condition] SOURCE — A memo call reaching its own unfinished key has no completed hit and runs under ordinary fuel; it does not reuse an unfinished result or stop early as unknown solely because that key is unfinished. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json; OQ3 A] The state-free predicate and memo-mark refusal rule are unchanged; the placement and proof of the additional reuse obligations remain E-13. No hit/miss tariff is selected by these decisions. SOURCE — Under Claude A's recorded M-C call, made under the owner's delegation, memo marks are permitted only on one-argument definitions. Ordinary several-argument calls continue. An author who wants such computation memoized passes one row explicitly to a one-argument definition; the evaluator does not automatically pack several arguments. Zero-argument memo waits and is outside this M-C profile. [SOURCE: owner-record-2026-11-12-memo-key-delegated-mc.json, authorizes_only; DECISIONS.postimage.md:39,51; BRIEF-MEMO-KEY.md:18,28,32-35.] PROPOSED — Scope the inherited unary identity key to the called definition and its single evaluated argument, so g(4) cannot reuse h(4)'s answer. The concrete representation of definition identity remains E-13. [SOURCE: owner-record-2026-11-12-memo-key-delegated-mc.json, authorizes_only; DECISIONS.postimage.md:39,51; BRIEF-MEMO-KEY.md:18,28,32-35.] Case: N10 W24, under EV-122–125 and eligible completed unary fresh-memo premises: memo g(y)=row(y,y); f(x)=let a=g(row(x)); g(row(x)), input4. Strict evaluation forces the first fill. The separately constructed immutable row(4) arguments have the same inherited evaluator identity. Identity-keyed cost25 returns row(row(4),row(4)); occurrence-keyed cost34. At fuel25 only the correct build returns, while the fault stops at the second miss. Across32 sensitivity settings the targeted fault spends5 through9 more; this is a conditional supplied witness, not complete memo/identity implementation. Excludes: The occurrence-keyed second call misses solely because the equal argument arose at a different occurrence. W24's distinct completion observation at the stated conditional budget rejects that fault. Do not claim a new canonical identity law, incomplete-entry result or unconditional tariff. PROPOSED — Concrete definition identity, completed installation, interrupted/warmed/resumed/restored states, retained-space/safe-bound accounts and exact fees remain OPEN. Planned M-A direct ordered keys with the reuse charge growing with argument count are E-13 widening work, to be taken up when a sketch or program shows a need; they are not current rules. Old records and kept points must retain their old profile, or receive separately reviewed migration and fresh evidence. Excludes: Using the ordinary sequence slot A=1 for natural 256; debiting a partial A+1; taking a count on rejected make; adding a primitive write entry on none. This rule does not turn arithmetic defaults, malformed admission or an unpayable huge-left-shift bound into ordinary none. On completion its new sequence has n positions holding the one evaluated value v; its sequence cell and trail entry are part of R. n=0 is a valid operation, not an edge. Figures are after the length and initializer evaluations, with new identity/count state committed only after payment. Excludes: Skipping initializer evaluation for zero length; treating make0 as a none edge; omitting its sequence/trail cells; consuming a count before an unaffordable or rejected operation commits. Text: PROPOSED A valid positional write invoices A=1+bits(i)+part(v), R=1, T=0 and W=1, so its own total is bits(i)+part(v)+3. Position size is charged in A, once, while R retains the trail-cell reservation; no earlier access enters any part. After full payment it performs the identity-preserving write, records the change and returns the same proposed affirmative result. Wrong kinds or outside range use Rule E and commit no write. SOURCE A sequence carries no marker. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json] Case: PROPOSED For an admitted length-3 sequence, i=2 and obtained v=7: A=1+2+3=6, R=1, T=0, W=1, total 8. Fuel 8 at the operation boundary commits the write; fuel 7 leaves this invoice unpaid with no write, trail entry or result construction, preserving earlier effects. For valid positions 7 and 8 with v=7 and other arguments fixed, the own totals are 9 and 10; W stays 1. Different prior access histories do not alter these totals. These omit argument evaluation and run-entry costs. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json] Excludes: PROPOSED A distance term, a second position-size debit, a total charge of only 1, omitted trail reservation, changed sequence identity, or a committed write/trail on an invalid or unpaid operation. No physical constant-time or whole-meter theorem follows. | EV-111 | NEW R6 source row; no inherited audit cells | NEW R6 listed-meter transcription | NEW R6 Arch METER R5 proposal | NEW R6 DRAFT, review follows | new | R16 Standing: retired — SOURCE the marker-only subject is removed by the later owner decision; identifier retained for provenance. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json] Text: SOURCE The marker-restoration action and its invoice are removed; no replacement zero-charge marker action is installed. PROPOSED Going back ranges only over the sequences reachable at the kept point, in sequence-name order; sequences made after the point are left out. For each such sequence, visit positions written since the point in ascending order and restore the value held at the point, skipping any position already holding that value, with each ordinary write charged as EV-110 before it occurs. There is one contents-restoration phase. Earlier paid steps and the full trail survive exhaustion, fuel is not refunded, and allocation high-water supply is not restored. Protection of restored positions and intervening observations, plus complete host/activation/use-once/concurrency contracts, remain owed. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json] Case: PROPOSED Restore a changed position only after its ordinary write invoice is paid. If a later write cannot be paid, keep the already restored prefix and its trail. There is no subsequent marker phase, including for a sequence that was only read. This is not an execution or a complete protocol proof. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-12-write-charge-arch-and-one-brief.json] Excludes: SOURCE / PROPOSED A marker invoice or hidden marker phase, refunded fuel, rewound creation supply, atomic rollback from these partial rules, or equal-budget replay without the charged-input/name premises. Global declarations are pairwise distinct and unreserved. Parameters cover the whole definition body; let binds only its continuation, not initializer; branch binds only its own body, and else nothing; both loop names bind only its body, not bound or initializer. No binder escapes. Each variable spelling is bound once per definition and is never global. Calls require primitive/definition role and exact arity; constructors and case heads require choice role. Missing/wrong-role globals and held words are refused before the run. Plain REF atoms use only visible binders, never the global table, and missing binders are refused. Forward global references are allowed without reordering declarations. Every case ordinal is below its choice count, none repeats, and else is required unless all cases are covered. Resolve once before running, to fixed references, never by runtime spelling lookup. Case: PROPOSED — S01 def f(x) = x resolves its local binder although x is absent from the global table. S02 let x = 0; x resolves only the continuation; S05 let x = x; x is refused for the initializer use. S04/S06–S09 refuse earlier, sibling/else, bound/initializer or escaped uses. N01 resolves a forward choice; N02 resolves a forward definition; N03 resolves a forward choice inside a literal. These are name derivations, not executions. Exclusions: PROPOSED — Global-table lookup of local variables; definition-wide binder visibility; shadowing/capture under this candidate; resolving only already-seen globals; silently reordering totality order; wrong-role/arity calls becoming ordinary none; runtime display-name rediscovery. | EV-121 | NEW R9 | NEW grammar/name/classifier family | conditional proposal | DRAFT; choices open | new DRAFT | Current R13: PROPOSED — For a resolved first-order definition f, its closure contains f and every definition called anywhere in any closure body, including all arguments, dead branches and recursive components. It passes exactly when no body names the fixed make, contents or write roles; unresolved callees have already been refused as text and cannot pass. Every make is forbidden even empty or unused. Immutable field/byte access has no sequence fallback; fixed length, same and handle carriage may pass without mutable-state observation. A paid immutable invocation outside its required operand domain returns none without primitive state changes, but valid reads can also return stored none. Sequence identities may occupy legal set member/query positions; a required set operand must still be a set. Prior argument effects stand, and an immutable outer call excuses none of its arguments. A failing memo mark refuses before the run. Every definition invoked by admission or result checking, including literal, constructor-field and membership checks and all callees, must pass marked or not. An unmarked stateful definition may be offered in an ordinary separately fuelled run under its other conditions; it has no admission exemption. This is exact for the syntactic forbidden-role predicate and conservative for semantic state independence; it is distinct from syntax-totality and provides no positive-loop equation. SOURCE — Computing a term once and reusing its result is permitted only when the term makes nothing new, has no effect, has no open choice, consumes nothing use-once, and has a result that may be copied. State-freedom alone is insufficient. [decision: owner-record-2026-11-11-web-oq1-oq3-a.json; OQ1 A with Arch condition] Gate PASS is only the stated effect predicate, not evidence of these additional reuse premises and not a new memo-mark refusal policy. [digest: DIGEST.json; F036] Case: PROPOSED — N9 C1–C13 are the thirteen explicit gate derivations: pure field/length/carriage/comparison and pure recursive closures pass; direct/helper/dead-branch contents, allocation and write fail. C12 has effect FAIL with no invalid mark to reject in the ordinary context, but fails admission/result eligibility. X2/X4 show that a safe outer operation or deep helper cannot hide forbidden work; X3 is a pure recursive component that passes only this effect predicate. No case claims successful evaluator execution or a complete fuel budget. Exclusions: PROPOSED — Path-pruning dead effects; omitting recursive/helper closure; pardoning argument effects; equating none with invalid operands in both directions; accepting a sequence as a required set receiver; applying the gate only to marked admission helpers; replacing syntactic conservatism by semantic completeness; treating gate PASS as totality, fuel success or adoption. A represented choice keeps its complete name/count identity. A sequence value holds its name, never its mutable contents. The proposal is prefix-free on canonical represented values.
